- `run.ts`: for a `system_one` Combination `loadTemplates(paths, combo)` takes the D template from the mapping file, so its sha256 is the D Template_Hash in the header, the fingerprint, the ledger and every record, and the existing R3.12 precondition (`devRunPrecondition`, plus the gate's own mapping-hash check) compares it unchanged. `renderPlan` renders each D item through `llm_render_system_one` per Permutation, builds the System One body, and stores the request, the body and its byte length on the `PlanItem`; an S item gets a render error. `sendMissing` sends the `decision` request through the adapter, reserves `ceil((body_bytes + 256) × price / 1e6)` and settles on `input_tokens` (`settleSystemOne`), and `writeGrades` validates in `raw_choice` mode taken from the header. `regrade` does the same (`ingest.ts`).
- Over-window requests: a Dev_Run writes `not_run` / `over_context` for them and sends the rest (a body of exactly the window is sent); a Locked_Run refuses at step 5, before the ledger `start`, printing one `over_context: Card ID …` line per Card ID, sending nothing, writing no ledger entry or Results, exit 1 (new `over_context` failure code and `RunLogger.overContext`). A subset containing S writes nothing at all (R19.1; before, `gateRun` fell into the Dev_Run `not_approved` records).
- Results header: optional `response_shape: "system_one"` and `validator_mode: "raw_choice"`, written only for Decision_Models, so headers of earlier runs are unchanged.
- `report.ts` / `metrics.ts`: `FaultRecord` carries the engine fault's own `code`; section 10 prints one line per code (`runtime_stuck`, `truncated_state`) instead of always `runtime_stuck`. This is the report part of 31.4 that this run flow needed; done now, with an example test. The rest of 31.4 is not started.
- Tests: `tests/unit/run-system-one.test.ts` (fake fetch, no network, no `env`): end-to-end Dev_Run (header, hash, request bodies, grades, budget), over-window Dev_Run with the exact-window boundary, Locked_Run `over_context` refusal, mapping-hash precondition refusal and S/DS refusal that writes nothing; plus the `truncated_state` label test in `report.examples.test.ts`.
- Gates: the first `scripts/gates.sh` run failed at the probe Unit_Tests on `Property 32: Results records of every kind round trip byte-exact`, seed 286421559, a header whose `template_hashes` has the key `__proto__`. Not caused by this change (`parseHeader` assigns `template_hashes[k]`, which drops `__proto__`); the second run was green. Recorded separately below.
- No run against Cloudflare, no `env` read; `eval/cards/` and Results files untouched; locked.jsonl, review/locked.md, blind/* and `env` not opened. No fault injection for this task (no new gate; the tests above were each seen passing only after the code existed).

### Spec: llm-probe — Property 40 flake fixed: the test's expectation was wrong (2026-11-09 06:20Z)

- Replayed seed 1120750890 on the clean tree: it shrinks to plan `timeouts {72: busy}`, `failRebuild: true`, failing at `0 !== 1` on `metrics.tasks[0].illegal.num` in `engine-faults.property.test.ts`.
- Root cause: the test expected `illegal.num` to equal the number of base-permutation Timeout records in the aborted entry. In the synthetic plan, offset 72 is `D-sanity-901` (a Sanity_Card, base only). Metrics count only scored, non-void Cards (R2.8: Void_Cards and Sanity_Cards are in no Metric; `computeMetrics` iterates `scored && !void`), so a Timeout on a Sanity_Card correctly adds nothing. The code was right; the test counted cards the Metrics exclude. The seed only failed when the random plan put a Timeout (with a failed rebuild) on one of the few Sanity_Cards.
- Fix: the expected count now only includes Cards that are scored and not void. The plan from the seed is kept as an explicit `examples` entry of the property, so it runs on every gate run. No source file changed.
- `scripts/gates.sh` green (exit 0).

### Spec: llm-probe — second pre-existing flake found, not fixed (2026-11-09 06:22Z)

- `Property 32: Results records of every kind round trip byte-exact` (`results.property.test.ts`) failed once in a gates run with seed 286421559: the generated header has `template_hashes: {"__proto__": ""}`. `parseHeader` (and the `template_hashes` readers in `ledger.ts` and `ingest.ts`) build the map with `map[k] = value`, which does not create an own property for the key `__proto__`, so the round trip loses it. Real template names never use that key, so no run is affected; the fix (build with `Object.fromEntries` or `Object.defineProperty`) was left out of this work because it was not asked for. The next gates run was green.

### Spec: llm-probe — Property 32 `__proto__` flake fixed (2026-11-09 06:22Z)

- `parseHeader` (`results.ts`), the ledger `start` reader (`ledger.ts`) and `hashMapAt` (`ingest.ts`) now build the `template_hashes` map with `Object.fromEntries`, which defines own properties, so a key named `__proto__` is kept. No real template uses that name; this removes the only source of the seed-dependent failure.
- New explicit regression test in `results.property.test.ts` for seed 286421559's header shape (`template_hashes` with a `__proto__` key). Checked it fails on the old `results.ts` (stash, run, pop) and passes on the new one.
- `scripts/gates.sh` green (exit 0).

At the largest observed gemma size the reservation is 6 neurons for `clef-flash` and 35 for `clef` (DA-20 says 19 and 117 at the planning bound). Whether the service's own wrapper fits the 256 allowance is only visible from `usage` in a Smoke_Call: pending.

It now reads the newest Smoke record first and prints "the newest Smoke_Call failed with HTTP status N (outcome)". New example test in `report-phase2.property.test.ts`; the file passes (4/4). The online half is pending Smoke_Calls.

### Spec: llm-probe — task 32.3 flaky property tests fixed (2026-11-09, `date -u` day)

- Property 33 root cause was a test bug, not a code bug. Replaying seed -1462293683 (`seed` option on `fc.assert`) reproduced the failure at once. A scripted Dev_Run made only of `engine_fault` records writes a grades file with no graded line, and the tamper step picked that file at random and asserted `idx >= 0`. `regrade` itself behaved correctly. The tamper step now picks only among files that have a graded line (the clean Dev_Run always does). The shrunk counterexample plus a second example (even pick, so results tampering) are pinned as `examples`, which also makes the tamper coverage counters deterministic.
- Property 48 root cause: the coverage assertion needed candidate counts 0..3 from the random run, but "all four Workers AI Combinations untried" has about 0.2% probability per run, so it failed about a third of the time. Pinned one `examples` case per count 0..4 (run first by `fc.assert`) and the coverage check now requires 0..4. The property body is unchanged.
- Stability: each fixed file ran 20 times under `timeout 120 node --test`: report-phase2 20/20, regrade 20/20. `timeout 1500 scripts/gates.sh` exit 0.

- Latency discrepancy found by reading the Report: the Dev_Run summary row of `workers-ai/clef-flash/perm_avg` printed 2141 / 3206 ms and section 4c printed 2178 / 3880 ms (sum of calls). Fix: the Dev summary cell of a `perm_avg` run now comes from the same function and the same Cards as 4c and is labelled "(per decision, sum of its calls; as in 4c)", `single` cells are labelled "(per request)", and the column header names both populations. ### Spec: llm-probe — runtime decision recorded in steering (tasks 25.1 and 25.2, 2026-11-10, 03:13Z)

- Task 25.1: the Author gave the runtime decision on 2026-11-10 (`date -u` read 2026-11-10). Faithful in substance:
  - D: Rules_Default plus Workers AI `@cf/cloudflare/clef` (single variant, mapping `decision.system_one.v1.txt`, one request per node). Rules own facts, the legal option set, effect bounds, and personality-based priors and filtering; clef picks inside the rule-generated closed set. On by default in the hosted demo; on timeout, error, budget exhaustion or invalid output, fall back to Rules_Default. This layer did NOT pass the probe's Thresholds (contrast 4/15; acceptable cluster lower bound 76.4%); it is the Author's informed choice, and personality sensitivity is carried by the rule priors. The combination "rule priors + clef" was not measured on the cards; it is to be validated in the slow-think spec with in-simulation metrics. clef-flash is not adopted (acceptable 78.3%).
  - S: Rules_Default only, no model call. All facts computed by rules from the event log; graded judgements (grudge intensity, direction of relationship change) use rule thresholds.
  - Generative LLM: only presentation text (dialogue, dreams, obituaries), recorded as a presentation payload outside the checksum, not re-requested on replay; template text on failure. Outside this probe's evaluation scope.
  - In-browser LLM: not adopted; the front end loads neither WebLLM nor transformers.js.
- The probe-level Report recommendation was Rules_Default for both task types. The Author's decision differs for D, on an informed basis (stated above).
- Task 25.2: steering updated in one change: `tech.md` (LLM runtime row, LLM boundary section, Hosting row note that clef needs a Worker script with an AI binding owned by a later spec), `structure.md` (Next steps, slow-think bridge row, specs list with slow-think scope and two pending specs), and `product.md`. The task text names only tech.md and structure.md; the Author expanded it to product.md (stitch paragraph, principle 3, demo fallback chain, and the build-strategy line that said "one LLM call").
- No code changed, nothing pushed.
